    36 . capture program drop copyOverleaf
      
    37 . program define copyOverleaf
        1.         syntax anything(name=fileName1), SUBdir(string asis)
        2.         * Make all slashes forward slashes
    38 .         
    39 .         local   betterFileName1 = subinstr(`fileName1', "\", "/", .)
        3.         
    40 .         local   destDir = "$overleaf/`subdir'"
        4. 
    41 . * Make destination filepath
    42 .         local fileName2 = "`destDir'/" + substr("`betterFileName1'", strrpos(
      > "`betterFileName1'", "/") + 1, .)
        5.         copy            `fileName1' "`fileName2'", replace
        6. end

   105 . * **********************************************************************
   106 . * Set choice variable
   107 . * **********************************************************************
   108 . cap program drop handle_choice
      
   109 . program define handle_choice
        1.     args choice
        2.     clonevar choice = `choice'
        3.     drop if mi(choice)
        4.     display as text "Note: Dropped `r(N_drop)' observations due to missing
      >  values in `choice'"
        5.     if "`choice'" == "nonag" label var choice "Non-Agricultural"
        6.     if "`choice'" == "urban" label var choice "Urban"
        7. end
      
   110 . 
   111 . * **********************************************************************
   112 . * Set dependent variable
   113 . * **********************************************************************
   114 . cap program drop handle_depvar
      
   115 . program define handle_depvar
        1.     args depvar
        2.     clonevar depvar = `depvar'
        3.     drop if mi(depvar) | depvar <= 0
        4.         display as text "Note: Dropped `r(N_drop)' observations due to mis
      > sing/negative values in `depvar' "
        5.     gen lndepvar = ln(depvar)
        6.         label var lndepvar "Log (`depvar')"
        7.         
   116 .         gen ln_income           = ln(income)
        8.         lab var                         ln_income "Log Income"
        9.         gen ln_consumption      = ln(consumption)
       10.         lab var                         ln_consumption "Log Consumption"
       11. end
      
   117 . 
   118 . * **********************************************************************
   119 . * Declare panel, impose balance if balance = bal
   120 . * **********************************************************************
   121 . cap program drop handle_balance
      
   122 . program define handle_balance
        1.     args balance
        2.     xtset pid period
        3.         local max_period = r(tmaxs)
        4.     by pid: gen nr_periods_obs  = _N            // number of obs for pid
        5.                 label var nr_periods_obs "Number of observations per indiv
      > idual"
        6.         gen unbalanced = (nr_periods_obs != `max_period')
        7.         gen unbalanced_choice = unbalanced*choice
        8.         lab var unbalanced "Unbalanced panel = 1"
        9.         lab var unbalanced_choice "Unbalanced panel * choice"
       10.     if "`balance'" == "bal" {
       11.                 keep if unbalanced == 0
       12.         }
       13. end
      
   123 . 
   124 . * **********************************************************************
   125 . * Handle trajectory groups
   126 . * **********************************************************************
   127 . cap program drop handle_trajectory_groups
      
   128 . program define handle_trajectory_groups
        1.   preserve
        2.         * Keep relevant observations and variables
   129 .         keep if !unbalanced
        3.         keep pid period choice
        4.         
   130 .         * Reshape the data
   131 .         reshape wide choice, i(pid) j(period)
        5.         
   132 .         * Initialize an empty string variable for trajectories
   133 .     gen string_traj = ""
        6.         
   134 .     * Unabbreviate the list of 'choice' variables
   135 .         unab choice_vars: choice*
        7.         
   136 .         * Concatenate choice variables into a single string
   137 .         foreach var of varlist `choice_vars' {
        8.                 tostring `var', gen(`var'S)
        9.                 replace string_traj = string_traj + `var'S
       10.                 drop `var'S  // Drop temporary string variables
       11.         }
       12. 
   138 .         * Encode string trajectories to numerical form, maintaining natural o
      > rder
   139 .         * it automatically orders them in a "natural" way (000, 001, ..., 110
      > , 111)
   140 .         * ordering matters for defining globals for 1st switcher, last switch
      > er, etc
   141 .     encode string_traj, gen(traj)
       13.     lab var traj "Trajectory indicators"
       14.     drop string_traj
       15. 
   142 .         * Save this as a tempfile for merging with original data
   143 .         drop choice*
       16.         tempfile traj
       17.         save `traj'
       18.         
   144 .         * Restore original data and merge with trajectories
   145 .         restore
       19.         merge m:1 pid using `traj', nogen
       20. 
   146 .         * Verify the trajectories (missing indicates unbalanced observations)
   147 .         rename traj trajectory
       21.         tab trajectory
       22.         * Grab biggest number (always-adopters)
   148 .         local max_trajectory = r(r)
       23.         gen switcher_temp = ((trajectory > 1 & trajectory < `max_trajector
      > y'))
       24.         bys pid: egen switcher = min(switcher_temp)
       25.         label variable switcher "Switcher"
       26.         gen non_switcher_temp = trajectory == 1 | trajectory == `max_traje
      > ctory'
       27.         bys pid: egen non_switcher = min(non_switcher_temp)
       28.         label variable non_switcher "Non-switcher"
       29.         drop switcher_temp non_switcher_temp
       30.         
   149 .         bysort pid: egen obs_per_individual = count(pid)
       31.         label variable obs_per_individual "Number of obs per pid"
       32.         
   150 .         bysort pid (year): g pid_first_obs = _n == 1
       33.         label variable pid_first_obs "Indicator for pid's first obs"
       34. end

       26.         label variable non_switcher_2waves "Non-switcher"

   201 . * **********************************************************************
   202 . * Time trend
   203 . * **********************************************************************
   204 . cap program drop gen_time_trend
      
   205 . program define gen_time_trend
        1.     sum year if period == 1
        2.     gen trend = year - r(min)
        3.         lab var trend "Time trend"
        4. end

   207 . * **********************************************************************
   208 . * gen_vfirst: earliest non-missing value of `vname' per pid
   209 . * **********************************************************************
   210 . * Used by initial_values_robust / run_grc_robust to build the
   211 . * time-invariant cluster index v_i (first-wave province/region) that
   212 . * VV (2020) Section F requires for the robust extrapolation.
   213 . * Unit-tested at explorations/verdier/3_test_gen_vfirst.do.
   214 . * The buggy bysort pid: egen min(cond(!missing(v), v, .)) returns the
   215 . * smallest numeric value of v per pid, NOT the first-wave value; the
   216 . * correct form below marks the FIRST non-missing row per pid sorted by
   217 . * year, then propagates its value within pid.
   218 . capture program drop gen_vfirst
      
   219 . program define gen_vfirst
        1.     syntax , vname(varname) genname(name)
        2.     capture drop `genname'
        3.     tempvar seq mark tmp
        4.     bysort pid (year): gen `seq' = sum(!missing(`vname'))
        5.     by pid: gen `mark' = (!missing(`vname')) & (`seq' == 1)
        6.     gen `tmp' = `vname' if `mark'
        7.     by pid: egen `genname' = max(`tmp')
        8.     lab var `genname' "First-wave non-missing `vname'"
        9. end

   224 . 
   225 . cap program drop set_covariates
      
   226 . program define set_covariates
        1.   args                  depvar country
        2.         gen                     loghhsize = log(hhsize) 
        3.         label var       loghhsize "Log Household Size"
        4. 
   227 .         gen                     rural = 1-urban
        5.         lab var         rural   "Rural"
        6. 
   228 .         if "`country'" ==       "IDN" {
        7.                 clonevar baseline_age = age1993
        8.     gen                         ag = 1-nonag
        9.     lab var     ag      "Agricultural"
       10.         }
       11.         if "`country'" == "CHN" | "`country'" == "CHN_hukou_rural_only" | 
      > "`country'" == "CHN_hukou_urban_only" | "`country'" == "CHN_hukou_rural_first
      > " | "`country'" == "CHN_hukou_urban_first" {
       12.                 clonevar baseline_age = age2010
       13.         }
       14.         if "`country'" ==       "TZA" {
       15.                 clonevar baseline_age = age2008
       16.         }
       17.         
   229 .         global  covs_1                                  "female"
       18.         global  covs_2                                  "$covs_1 c.age#c.a
      > ge"
       19.         global  covs_all                                "$covs_2 c.educati
      > on_max##c.education_max"
       20.         
   230 .         global  covs_1_hukou                    "hukou"
       21.         global  covs_2_hukou                    "$covs_1_hukou female"
       22.         global  covs_3_hukou                    "$covs_2_hukou c.age#c.age
      > "
       23.         global  covs_all_hukou                  "$covs_3_hukou c.education
      > _max##c.education_max"
       24.         
   231 .         * gmm doesn't play nice with some factor variables
   232 .         * so reluctantly generating the interaction terms
   233 .         * factor variables also drastically increase the computation time for
      >  gmm
   234 .         gen baseline_age2  = baseline_age*baseline_age
       25.     gen age2           = age*age
       26.         gen education_max2 = education_max*education_max
       27.         global covs_gmm   "female"
       28. //      global covs_gmm2  "$covs_gmm baseline_age baseline_age2"
   235 .     global covs_gmm2  "$covs_gmm age2"
       29. 
   236 .         global covs_gmm_all "$covs_gmm2 education_max education_max2"
       30.         
   237 .         global covs_gmm_hukou           "hukou"
       31.         global covs_gmm2_hukou          "$covs_gmm_hukou female"
       32.     global covs_gmm3_hukou              "$covs_gmm2_hukou age2"
       33.         global covs_gmm_all_hukou       "$covs_gmm3_hukou education_max ed
      > ucation_max2"
       34. 
   238 .   drop if mi(education_max)
       35.         drop if mi(age)
       36.         drop if obs_per_individual == 1
       37. 
   239 . end
      
   240 . * **********************************************************************
   241 . * Variable labels
   242 . * **********************************************************************
   243 . cap program drop        fix_varlabels
      
   244 . program define          fix_varlabels
        1.         lab variable          baseline_age              "Age at baseline (
      > years)"
        2.         lab variable          baseline_age2     "Age at baseline (years) s
      > quared"
        3.   lab variable          age             "Age (years)"
        4.   lab variable          age2            "Age (years) squared"
        5.         lab variable          education_max     "Education (years)"
        6.         lab variable          education_max2    "Education (years) squared
      > "
        7.         lab variable          hhsize          "Household Size"
        8.         lab variable          female          "Female"
        9.         capture: lab variable nonag           "Non-Agricultural"
       10. end
      
   245 . 
   246 . * **********************************************************************
   247 . * Custom LaTeX table (for summary stats by country)
   248 . * **********************************************************************
   249 . * Create a custom LaTeX table
   250 . cap program             drop sumstats_table
      
   251 . program                                 define sumstats_table
        1.         syntax, TABle_notes(string asis) COUNTRY(string asis) OUTputdir(st
      > ring asis) FILEname(string asis) BALance(string asis)
        2.         
   252 .         if "`country'"  == "IDN" {
        3.                 local           country_name "Indonesia"
        4.         }
        5.         if "`country'"  == "CHN" {
        6.                 local           country_name "China"
        7.         }
        8.         if "`country'"  == "TZA" {
        9.                 local           country_name "Tanzania"
       10.         }        
       11.   local       balance_caption ""
       12.   if "`balance'"  == "bal" {
       13.       local   balance_caption ", Balanced Panel"
       14.   }
       15.   
   253 .         * Clear any existing file handle
   254 .         file close _all
       16. 
   255 .   * Construct the full file path using the output directory
   256 .   local filepath "`outputdir'/`filename'.tex"
       17.         local table_label "tab:`filename'"
       18. 
   257 .   file open myfile using "`filepath'", write replace
       19.         if "`choice'"  == "urban" & "`balance'"  == "unb" {
       20.                 file write myfile "\begin{table}[h!] \centering \caption{S
      > ummary Statistics `country_name'`balance_caption'}\label{`table_label'} \begi
      > n{threeparttable} \begin{tabular}{l cccc} \toprule" _n
       21.         }
       22.         else {
       23.                 file write myfile "\begin{table}[htbp] \centering \caption
      > {Summary Statistics `country_name'`balance_caption'}\label{`table_label'} \be
      > gin{threeparttable} \begin{tabular}{l cccc} \toprule" _n
       24.         }
       25.         if "`choice'"  == "urban" {
       26.       file write myfile "& All & Rural & Urban & Difference \\" _n
       27.         }
       28.         if "`choice'"  == "nonag" {
       29.       file write myfile "& All & Agricultural & Non-Agricultural & Differe
      > nce \\" _n
       30.         }
       31.         file write myfile " &  &  &  & \$t\$-test \\" _n
       32.         /* file write myfile "\addlinespace" _n */
   258 .         file write myfile "\midrule" _n
       33. 
   259 .         * Loop through each observation and write to the LaTeX file
   260 .         quietly {
       34.                 forvalues i = 1/`=_N' {
       35.                         local varname             = v1[`i']
       36.                         local all                                 = v3[`i'
      > ]
       37.                         local rural                       = v5[`i']
       38.                         local urban                       = v7[`i']
       39.                         local difference                = v9[`i']
       40.                         * Add one linespace after Location
   261 .                         if `i' == 1 {
       41.                                 file write myfile "`varname' & `all' & `ru
      > ral' & `urban' & `difference' \\  \addlinespace \addlinespace" _n
       42.                         }
       43.                         else {
       44.                                 * Only add \addlinespace to odd lines, exc
      > ept for "Observations" row
   262 .                                 if mod(`i', 2) == 1 & `i' != 15 {
       45.                                         file write myfile "`varname' & `al
      > l' & `rural' & `urban' & `difference' \\  \addlinespace" _n
       46.                                 }
       47.                                 else {
       48.                                                 file write myfile "`varnam
      > e' & `all' & `rural' & `urban' & `difference' \\" _n
       49.                                 }
       50.                         }
       51.                 }
       52.         }
       53. 
   263 .         file write myfile "\bottomrule \end{tabular} \begin{tablenotes}[flush
      > left] \footnotesize \item{`table_notes'} \end{tablenotes} \end{threeparttable
      > } \end{table}"
       54.         file close myfile
       55.                 
   264 . end
      
   265 . 
   266 . * **********************************************************************
   267 . * Summary stats table prep
   268 . * **********************************************************************
   269 . cap program drop                country_summary_stats
      
   270 . program define                  country_summary_stats
        1. args                                                            country ch
      > oice depvar balance
        2.         
   271 . * Grab number of observations   
   272 .         summarize                                       pid
        3.         local observations                      = r(N)
        4. * Format the observations to have a comma
   273 .         local observations_formatted = string(`observations', "%9.0fc") 
        5. * Grab number of individuals
   274 .         egen tag                                        = tag(pid)
        6.         summarize                                       tag
        7.         local num_individuals           = r(sum)
        8.         local num_individuals_formatted = string(`num_individuals', "%9.0f
      > c")           
        9. * Grab number of obs for urban/rural
   275 .         count if rural
       10.         local num_rural                 = r(N)  
       11.         local num_rural_formatted = string(`num_rural', "%9.0fc")         
      >       
       12.         count if urban
       13.         local num_urban                 = r(N)  
       14.         local num_urban_formatted = string(`num_urban', "%9.0fc")         
      >       
       15. * Grab percentages of urban and rural
   276 .         summarize                                       rural
       16.         local percent_rural                     = r(mean)*100
       17.         local percent_rural_formatted : display %6.1f `percent_rural' "\%"
       18.         summarize                                       urban
       19.         local percent_urban                     = r(mean)*100
       20.         local percent_urban_formatted : display %6.1f `percent_urban' "\%"
      >       
       21. * Grab percent non-switchers    
   277 .         summarize                                       non_switcher if pid_f
      > irst_obs == 1
       22.         local non_switchers                     = r(mean)*100
       23.         local non_switchers_formatted : display %6.1f `non_switchers' "\%"
      >       
       24.                         
   278 . * Create summary stats table, export as .csv file       
   279 .         iebaltab                rural ln_consumption ln_income female age    
      >              ///
      >                                         education_max hhsize, ///
      >                                         savecsv("summary_stats_`country'_`bal
      > ance'.csv") replace  ///
      >                                         groupvar(urban) total ///
      >                                         totallabel(All) format(%9.2fc) ///
      >                                         rowvarlabels stats(desc(sd)) nonote
       25.         
   280 . * Import the saved CSV file
   281 .         import delimited using summary_stats_`country'_`balance'.csv, clear  
      >    
       26.         
   282 . * Drop unnecessary columns & rows
   283 .         drop v2 v4 v6 v8
       27.         drop in 1/3
       28.         drop in 2/2
       29.         set obs 19
       30.         replace v1              = "Location"                    in 1
       31.         replace v3              = ""                                    in
      >  1
       32.         replace v5              = "`percent_rural_formatted'" in 1
       33.         replace v7              = "`percent_urban_formatted'" in 1
       34.         replace v9              = ""                                    in
      >  1
       35.         replace v1              = "\cmidrule{2-5}"              in 16
       36.         replace v1              = "Observations"                in 17
       37.         replace v1              = "Individuals"                 in 18
       38.         replace v1              = "Non-switchers"               in 19
       39.         replace v3              = "`observations_formatted'"            in
      >  17   
       40.         replace v3              = "`num_individuals_formatted'"         in
      >  18
       41.         replace v3              = "`non_switchers_formatted'"   in 19
       42.         replace v5              = "`num_rural_formatted'"                 
      >       in 17
       43.         replace v7              = "`num_urban_formatted'"                 
      >       in 17
       44. end

   342 . 
   343 . * **********************************************************************
   344 . * Clean up .tex files
   345 . * **********************************************************************
   346 . capture program drop removeStringFromTex
      
   347 . program define removeStringFromTex
        1.     syntax anything(name=texFileName) , REMove(string asis)
        2. 
   348 .     * Create a temporary file to store the modified content
   349 .     tempfile tempFile
        3. 
   350 .     * Use filefilter to remove the specified string
   351 .     filefilter `texFileName' `tempFile', from(`remove') to("")
        4. 
   352 .     * Replace the original file with the modified file
   353 .     copy `tempFile' `texFileName', replace
        5. end
      
   354 . 
   355 . * **********************************************************************
   356 . * Create three-part LaTeX table
   357 . * **********************************************************************
   358 . cap program drop create_panel_tex_table
      
   359 . program define create_panel_tex_table
        1.     syntax ,    Panels(integer) COLumns(integer) FILEname(string asis)  //
      > /
      >                                 COUNTRIES(string asis) Keep(varlist)    ///
      >                                 PREhead(string asis) POSTfoot(string asis) //
      > /
      >                                 COEFLABels(string asis) TEXTdepvar(string asi
      > s)
        2.         
   360 .     // Split the panel names, prehead, and postfoot strings into tokens
   361 . 
   362 .     local num_panels `panels'
        3.     local ccc ""
        4.     * Loop to concatenate "c" the number of times specified in `columns'
   363 .     forval i = 1/`columns' {
        5.         local ccc "`ccc'c"
        6.     }
        7.     local cmid = `columns' + 1
        8.     
   364 .         forvalues i = 1/`num_panels' {
        9.                 local replace append
       10.                 local colnumbers ""
       11.                 local table_prehead     ""
       12.                 local table_postfoot    ""
       13.                 local posthead                  ""
       14.                 if `i' == 1 & `i' == `num_panels' {
       15.                         local replace replace
       16.                         local table_prehead1 "`"\begin{table}[htbp] \cente
      > ring \begin{threeparttable}"'"
       17.                         local table_prehead2 "`"\begin{tabular}{l `ccc'} \
      > toprule  \textbf{Dep. var:} `textdepvar'"'"
       18.                         local table_prehead "`table_prehead1' `prehead' `t
      > able_prehead2'"
       19.                         local table_posthead "\textbf{Panel A: Indonesia} 
      > \\"
       20.                         local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2 -`cmid'} `postfo
      > ot'"
       21.                 }
       22.                 if `i' == 1 & `i' != `num_panels' {
       23.                         local replace replace
       24.                         local table_prehead1 "`"\begin{table}[htbp] \cente
      > ring \begin{threeparttable}"'"
       25.                         local table_prehead2 "`"\begin{tabular}{l `ccc'} \
      > toprule  \textbf{Dep. var:} `textdepvar'"'"
       26.                         local table_prehead "`table_prehead1' `prehead' `t
      > able_prehead2'"
       27.                         local table_posthead "\textbf{Panel A: Indonesia} 
      > \\"
       28.                         local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2 -`cmid'}"
       29.                 }
       30.                 if `i' == 2 {
       31.                         local colnumbers nonum
       32.                         local table_posthead "\textbf{Panel B: China} \\"
       33.                         local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2-`cmid'}"
       34.                 }
       35.                 if `i' == 3 {
       36.                         local colnumbers nonum
       37.                         local table_posthead "\textbf{Panel C: Tanzania} \
      > \"
       38.                         local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2-`cmid'} `postfoo
      > t'"
       39.                 }
       40. 
   365 .                 local panelname `i'
       41.     local ests = ""        
       42.                 
   366 .                 // Extract word i of panelnames
   367 .                 local country : word `i' of `countries'
       43.                 di as error "Panel `i' `country'"
       44.                 
   368 .         // Generate the list of stored estimates for the current panel
   369 .         forvalues j = 1/`columns' {
       45.             local ests = "`ests' reg`j'_`country'"
       46.         }
       47.         
   370 .         esttab `ests'                          ///
      >         using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >                 se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >         keep(`keep') fragment booktabs         ///
      >         collabels("")                          ///
      >         starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >                 s(N N_clust r2_a, label("Observations" "Individuals" "Adj. R\
      > $^{2}\$") ///
      >         fmt(%9.0fc %9.0fc a2))                 ///
      >         varwidth(20)                           ///
      >         nolines nomtitles `colnumbers'         ///
      >         coeflabels(`coeflabels')               ///
      >         prehead(`table_prehead')               ///
      >         posthead(`table_posthead')             ///
      >         postfoot("`table_postfoot'")           ///
      >         `replace' substitute(\_ _)
       48.     }
       49. 
   371 . end

   373 . * **********************************************************************
   374 . * Create three-part LaTeX table - learning IDN
   375 . * **********************************************************************
   376 . cap program drop create_panel_tex_table_learn_IDN
      
   377 . program define create_panel_tex_table_learn_IDN
        1.     syntax ,    COLumns(integer) FILEname(string asis)  ///
      >                                 Keep(varlist)   ///
      >                                 PREhead(string asis) POSTfoot(string asis) //
      > /
      >                                 COEFLABels(string asis) TEXTdepvar(string asi
      > s)
        2.         
   378 .     // Split the panel names, prehead, and postfoot strings into tokens
   379 . 
   380 .     local ccc ""
        3.     * Loop to concatenate "c" the number of times specified in `columns'
   381 .     forval i = 1/`columns' {
        4.         local ccc "`ccc'c"
        5.     }
        6.     local cmid = `columns' + 1
        7.     
   382 .         local replace append
        8.         local colnumbers ""
        9.         local table_prehead     ""
       10.         local table_postfoot    ""
       11.         local posthead                  ""
       12.         local replace replace
       13.         local table_prehead1 "`"\begin{table}[htbp] \centering \begin{thre
      > eparttable}"'"
       14.         local table_prehead2 "`"\begin{tabular}{l `ccc'} \toprule  \textbf
      > {Dep. var:} `textdepvar'"'"
       15.         local table_prehead "`table_prehead1' `prehead' `table_prehead2'"
       16.         local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2 -`cmid'} `postfoot'"
       17. 
   383 .         local ests = ""        
       18.                 
   384 .         // Generate the list of stored estimates for the current panel
   385 .     forvalues j = 1/`columns' {
       19.                 local ests = "`ests' reg`j'_IDN"
       20.     }
       21.         
   386 .     esttab `ests'                          ///
      >     using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >         se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >     keep(`keep') fragment booktabs         ///
      >     collabels("")                          ///
      >     starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >         s(urban_p urban_2 urban_3 urban_4 rural_p rural_2 rural_3 rural_4 N N
      > _clust r2_a, label("Joint p-value urban learning" "p-value: 1 year urban = 2 
      > years urban" "p-value: 1 year urban = 3 years urban" "p-value: 1 year urban =
      >  4 years urban" "Joint p-value rural learning" "p-value: 1 year rural = 2 yea
      > rs rural" "p-value: 1 year rural = 3 years rural" "p-value: 1 year rural = 4 
      > years rural" "Observations" "Individuals" "Adj. R\$^{2}\$") ///
      >     fmt(%9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.3fc %9.0fc %9.0fc
      >  a2))      ///
      >     varwidth(20)                           ///
      >     nolines nomtitles `colnumbers'         ///
      >     coeflabels(`coeflabels')               ///
      >     prehead(`table_prehead')               ///
      >     postfoot("`table_postfoot'")           ///
      >     `replace' substitute(\_ _)
       22. 
   387 . end

   405 . * **********************************************************************
   406 . * OLS regressions
   407 . * **********************************************************************
   408 . cap program drop reghdfe_regressions
      
   409 . program define reghdfe_regressions
        1.     args country choice depvar balance
        2.     * OLS / FE regressions using reghdfe
   410 .         * Run col 7 first as it has the smallest sample, then use e(sample)
   411 .     eststo reg7_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice                           
      >                                    ///
      >                                 $covs_all                                    
      >                                                                            //
      > /
      >                                 , vce(cluster pid) absorb(pid period)
        3.                 gen regression_sample = e(sample)
        4.         
   412 .     eststo reg1_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice                           
      >                                    ///
      >                 if regression_sample, noabsorb vce(cluster pid)
        5.           eststo reg2_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice                  
      >                                       ///
      >                 if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
        6.     eststo reg3_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice $covs_1                
      >                               ///
      >                 if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
        7.           eststo reg4_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice $covs_2          
      >                               ///
      >                 if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
        8.     eststo reg5_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice $covs_all              
      >                       ///
      >                 if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
        9.     eststo reg6_`country': reghdfe lndepvar choice $covs_all              
      >                       ///
      >                 if regression_sample & switcher, vce(cluster pid) absorb(peri
      > od)
       10. end

   414 . * **********************************************************************
   415 . * OLS regressions (learning)
   416 . * **********************************************************************
   417 . cap program drop reghdfe_regressions_learn_IDN
      
   418 . program define reghdfe_regressions_learn_IDN
        1.     args country depvar balance
        2.     * OLS / FE regressions using reghdfe
   419 .         * Run col 4 first as it has the smallest sample, then use e(sample)
   420 .     eststo reg4_IDN: reghdfe lndepvar urban_1period urban_2period urban_3peri
      > od urban_4period rural_1period rural_2period rural_3period rural_4period $cov
      > s_all , vce(cluster pid) absorb(pid period)
        3.         gen regression_sample = e(sample)
        4.         test urban_1period=urban_2period=urban_3period=urban_4period, mtes
      > t
        5.         local urban_p = r(p)
        6.         local urban_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
        7.         local urban_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
        8.         local urban_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
        9.         test rural_1period=rural_2period=rural_3period=rural_4period, mtes
      > t
       10.         local rural_p = r(p)
       11.         local rural_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       12.         local rural_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       13.         local rural_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       14.         estadd scalar urban_p = `urban_p'
       15.         estadd scalar urban_2 = `urban_1_2'
       16.         estadd scalar urban_3 = `urban_1_3'
       17.         estadd scalar urban_4 = `urban_1_4'
       18.         estadd scalar rural_p = `rural_p'
       19.         estadd scalar rural_2 = `rural_1_2'
       20.         estadd scalar rural_3 = `rural_1_3'
       21.         estadd scalar rural_4 = `rural_1_4'
       22. 
   421 .     eststo reg1_IDN: reghdfe lndepvar urban_1period urban_2period urban_3peri
      > od urban_4period rural_1period rural_2period rural_3period rural_4period   if
      >  regression_sample, noabsorb vce(cluster pid)
       23.         test urban_1period=urban_2period=urban_3period=urban_4period, mtes
      > t
       24.         local urban_p = r(p)
       25.         local urban_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       26.         local urban_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       27.         local urban_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       28.         test rural_1period=rural_2period=rural_3period=rural_4period, mtes
      > t
       29.         local rural_p = r(p)
       30.         local rural_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       31.         local rural_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       32.         local rural_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       33.         estadd scalar urban_p = `urban_p'
       34.         estadd scalar urban_2 = `urban_1_2'
       35.         estadd scalar urban_3 = `urban_1_3'
       36.         estadd scalar urban_4 = `urban_1_4'
       37.         estadd scalar rural_p = `rural_p'
       38.         estadd scalar rural_2 = `rural_1_2'
       39.         estadd scalar rural_3 = `rural_1_3'
       40.         estadd scalar rural_4 = `rural_1_4'
       41.         
   422 .         eststo reg2_IDN: reghdfe lndepvar urban_1period urban_2period urban_3
      > period urban_4period rural_1period rural_2period rural_3period rural_4period 
      > if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
       42.         test urban_1period=urban_2period=urban_3period=urban_4period, mtes
      > t
       43.         local urban_p = r(p)
       44.         local urban_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       45.         local urban_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       46.         local urban_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       47.         test rural_1period=rural_2period=rural_3period=rural_4period, mtes
      > t
       48.         local rural_p = r(p)
       49.         local rural_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       50.         local rural_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       51.         local rural_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       52.         estadd scalar urban_p = `urban_p'
       53.         estadd scalar urban_2 = `urban_1_2'
       54.         estadd scalar urban_3 = `urban_1_3'
       55.         estadd scalar urban_4 = `urban_1_4'
       56.         estadd scalar rural_p = `rural_p'
       57.         estadd scalar rural_2 = `rural_1_2'
       58.         estadd scalar rural_3 = `rural_1_3'
       59.         estadd scalar rural_4 = `rural_1_4'
       60.     
   423 .         eststo reg3_IDN: reghdfe lndepvar urban_1period urban_2period urban_3
      > period urban_4period rural_1period rural_2period rural_3period rural_4period 
      > $covs_all if regression_sample, vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
       61.         test urban_1period=urban_2period=urban_3period=urban_4period, mtes
      > t
       62.         local urban_p = r(p)
       63.         local urban_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       64.         local urban_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       65.         local urban_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       66.         test rural_1period=rural_2period=rural_3period=rural_4period, mtes
      > t
       67.         local rural_p = r(p)
       68.         local rural_1_2 = r(mtest)[1,3]
       69.         local rural_1_3 = r(mtest)[2,3]
       70.         local rural_1_4 = r(mtest)[3,3]
       71.         estadd scalar urban_p = `urban_p'
       72.         estadd scalar urban_2 = `urban_1_2'
       73.         estadd scalar urban_3 = `urban_1_3'
       74.         estadd scalar urban_4 = `urban_1_4'
       75.         estadd scalar rural_p = `rural_p'
       76.         estadd scalar rural_2 = `rural_1_2'
       77.         estadd scalar rural_3 = `rural_1_3'
       78.         estadd scalar rural_4 = `rural_1_4'
       79.                 
   424 . end

   434 . 
   435 . * **********************************************************************
   436 . * Get ready for GRC
   437 . * **********************************************************************
   438 . 
   439 . cap program drop setup_grc_estimation
      
   440 . program define setup_grc_estimation
        1.     global              never 1
        2.     qui: tab                    trajectory
        3.     global              always `r(r)'   // Last trajectory
        4.     local               lastswitcher = $always-1
        5.     numlist     "2(1)`lastswitcher'"    // Grab list of switchers
        6.     global              switchers `r(numlist)'
        7.     numlist     "1(1)`lastswitcher'"    // Grab list of all-but-always
        8.     global              noalways `r(numlist)'
        9.     global              last = $always-1
       10.     global              first = $never+1
       11. 
   441 .     replace trajectory = 999 if trajectory == . // Unbalanced
       12. 
   442 .     * Generating dummies for gmm
   443 .     gen always = (trajectory == $always)
       13.     gen always_choice = always*choice
       14. 
   444 .     gen never = (trajectory == 1)
       15. 
   445 .     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       16.       gen switcher_`s' = (trajectory == `s')
       17.       gen switcher_`s'_choice = switcher_`s'*choice
       18.     }
       19.     
   446 . end
      
   447 . 
   448 . * **********************************************************************
   449 . * Make heterogeneity figures
   450 . * **********************************************************************
   451 . cap program drop heterogeneity_plots
      
   452 . program define heterogeneity_plots
        1.         args country choice depvar balance
        2.         if "`country'" == "IDN" {
        3.                 local textcountry "Indonesia"
        4.         }
        5.         if "`country'" == "CHN" {
        6.                 local textcountry "China"
        7.         }
        8.         if "`country'" == "TZA" {
        9.                 local textcountry "Tanzania"
       10.         }
       11. 
   453 . * Compute \Delta estimates for switchers centered around \Delta_never estimat
      > e
   454 . * Without covariates
   455 .   eststo nocovars_`country':    reg lndepvar i.trajectory                    
      >                                        ///
      >                                                                              
      >                                            i($switchers).trajectory#i.choice 
      >                       ///
      >                                                                              
      >                                            i.unbalanced#i.choice             
      >                               ///
      >                                                                              
      >                                            , vce(cluster pid)
       12. 
   456 . * F-test of equality for \mu
   457 .         testparm i($switchers).trajectory, equal
       13.   estadd sca F_mus_`country'_nocovars = r(F), replace
       14.         estadd sca p_mus_`country'_nocovars = r(p), replace
       15.         local F_mus_`country'_nocovars: di %-6.2f r(F)
       16.         local p_mus_`country'_nocovars: di %-3.2f r(p)
       17. 
   458 . * F-test of equality for \Delta
   459 .         testparm i($switchers).trajectory#1.choice, equal
       18.         estadd sca F_dts_`country'_nocovars = r(F), replace
       19.         estadd sca p_dts_`country'_nocovars = r(p), replace
       20.         local F_dts_`country'_nocovars: di %-6.2f r(F)
       21.         local p_dts_`country'_nocovars: di %-3.2f r(p)
       22. 
   460 . * With covariates for \Delta
   461 .   eststo covars_`country':        reghdfe lndepvar i.trajectory              
      >                                            ///
      >                                                                              
      >                                      i($switchers).trajectory#i.choice       
      >               ///
      >                                                                   i.unbalance
      > d#i.choice $covs_all         ///
      >                                                                              
      >                                      , vce(cluster pid) absorb(period)
       23. 
   462 . * F-test of equality for \mu
   463 .         testparm i($switchers).trajectory, equal
       24.   estadd sca F_mus_`country'_covars = r(F), replace
       25.         estadd sca p_mus_`country'_covars = r(p), replace
       26.         local F_mus_`country'_covars: di %-6.2f r(F)
       27.         local p_mus_`country'_covars: di %-3.2f r(p)
       28. 
   464 . * F-test of equality for \Delta
   465 .         testparm i($switchers).trajectory#1.choice, equal
       29.         estadd sca F_dts_`country'_covars = r(F), replace
       30.         estadd sca p_dts_`country'_covars = r(p), replace
       31.         local F_dts_`country'_covars: di %-6.2f r(F)
       32.         local p_dts_`country'_covars: di %-3.2f r(p)
       33.                           
   466 . * Grab interaction coefficients to plot for \Delta
   467 .   local interaction_coefs ""
       34.   local coefnames: colnames e(b)
       35.   foreach coef of local coefnames {
       36.         * if the coefname contains #1.choice
   468 .     if strpos("`coef'", "#1.choice") {
       37.       local interaction_coefs "`interaction_coefs' `coef'"
       38.                 }
       39. }
       40. 
   469 . * Grab coefficients to plot for \mu
   470 .   local mu_coefs ""
       41.   foreach coef of local coefnames {
       42.         * if the coefname is a trajectory without choice
   471 .     if regexm("`coef'", "trajectory$") {
       43.       local mu_coefs "`mu_coefs' `coef'"
       44.                 }
       45. }
       46. 
   472 . * Set truncation length for coeflabels
   473 .         if "`country'" == "IDN" {
       47.                 local coefnr    5
       48.         }
       49.         if "`country'" == "CHN" {
       50.                 local coefnr            4
       51.         }
       52.         if "`country'" == "TZA" {
       53.                 local coefnr    3
       54.         }
       55.         
   474 . * Plot them for \Delta, sorting by size
   475 .         coefplot ///
      >                 (covars_`country', keep(`interaction_coefs') msymb(S) mcolor(
      > lavender%100) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%100)))          ///
      >                 (nocovars_`country', keep(`interaction_coefs') msymb(O) mcolo
      > r(lavender%50) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%50)))  ///
      >                 , sort coeflabels(, truncate(`coefnr')) grid(none) ///  
      >                 legend(order(2 "All covariates & time FE" 4 "No covariates") 
      > pos(6) rows(1) size(small) span)           ///
      >                 ysize(10) xsize(8)                                           
      >    ///
      >                 ylabel(, labsize(small)) xlabel(, labsize(small))            
      >                                    ///
      >                 xline(0, lwidth(thin) lcolor(%40)) title("`textcountry'") ///
      >                 note("F-stat (all equal): `F_dts_`country'_covars' (p-value: 
      > `p_dts_`country'_covars')", size(small) pos(6) span) ///
      >                 saving(hetplotDelta_`depvar'_`choice'_`balance'_`country'_Fco
      > vars, replace)
       56.         
   476 .                 graph save "$output/figures/hetplotDelta_`depvar'_`choice'_`b
      > alance'_`country'_Fcovars.pdf", replace
       57.         
   477 .         coefplot ///
      >                 (covars_`country', keep(`interaction_coefs') msymb(S) mcolor(
      > lavender%100) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%100)))          ///
      >                 (nocovars_`country', keep(`interaction_coefs') msymb(O) mcolo
      > r(lavender%50) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%50)))  ///
      >                 , sort coeflabels(, truncate(`coefnr')) grid(none) ///  
      >                 legend(order(2 "All covariates & time FE" 4 "No covariates") 
      > pos(6) rows(1) size(small) span)           ///
      >                 ysize(10) xsize(8)                                           
      >    ///
      >                 ylabel(, labsize(small)) xlabel(, labsize(small))            
      >                                    ///
      >                 xline(0, lwidth(thin) lcolor(%40)) title("`textcountry'") ///
      >                 note("F-stat (all equal): `F_dts_`country'_nocovars' (p-value
      > : `p_dts_`country'_nocovars')", size(small) pos(6) span) ///
      >                 saving(hetplotDelta_`depvar'_`choice'_`balance'_`country'_Fno
      > covars, replace)
       58.         
   478 .                 graph save "$output/figures/hetplotDelta_`depvar'_`choice'_`b
      > alance'_`country'_Fnocovars.pdf", replace
       59.         
   479 . * Plot them for \mu, sorting by size
   480 .         coefplot ///
      >                 (covars_`country', keep(`mu_coefs') msymb(S) mcolor(lavender%
      > 100) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%100)))   ///
      >                 (nocovars_`country', keep(`mu_coefs') msymb(O) mcolor(lavende
      > r%50) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%50)))   ///
      >                 , sort coeflabels(, truncate(`coefnr')) grid(none) ///  
      >                 legend(order(2 "All covariates & time FE" 4 "No covariates") 
      > pos(6) rows(1) size(small) span)           ///

      >                                    ///
      >                 xline(0, lwidth(thin) lcolor(%40)) title("`textcountry'") ///
      >                 note("F-stat (all equal): `F_mus_`country'_covars' (p-value: 
      > `p_mus_`country'_covars')", size(small) pos(6) span) ///
      >                 saving(hetplotmu_`depvar'_`choice'_`balance'_`country'_Fcovar
      > s, replace)
       60.         
   481 .                 graph save "$output/figures/hetplotmu_`depvar'_`choice'_`bala
      > nce'_`country'_Fcovars.pdf", replace
       61.         
   482 .         coefplot ///
      >                 (covars_`country', keep(`mu_coefs') msymb(S) mcolor(lavender%
      > 100) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%100)))   ///
      >                 (nocovars_`country', keep(`mu_coefs') msymb(O) mcolor(lavende
      > r%50) ciopts(lwidth(thick) lcolor(lavender%50)))   ///
      >                 , sort coeflabels(, truncate(`coefnr')) grid(none) ///  
      >                 legend(order(2 "All covariates & time FE" 4 "No covariates")

      >                                    ///
      >                 xline(0, lwidth(thin) lcolor(%40)) title("`textcountry'") ///
      >                 note("F-stat (all equal): `F_mus_`country'_nocovars' (p-value
      > : `p_mus_`country'_nocovars')", size(small) pos(6) span) ///
      >                 saving(hetplotmu_`depvar'_`choice'_`balance'_`country'_Fnocov
      > ars, replace)
       62.         
   483 .                 graph save "$output/figures/hetplotmu_`depvar'_`choice'_`bala
      > nce'_`country'_Fnocovars.pdf", replace
       63.
